<div class="wotd" id="wotd">
<div class="wotd-label">📖 Word of the day</div>
<div class="wotd-word">baptism of fire <span class="wotd-pos">noun</span></div>
<div class="wotd-def">A baptism of fire (or baptism by fire, as it is sometimes referred to in the U.S.) is an introductory or initial experience that is a severe ordeal. In military contexts, baptism of fire specifically refers to a soldier&#x27;s first exposure to enemy fire.</div>
<div class="wotd-ex">The newly recruited player faced a baptism of fire as the team went head to head with their greatest rival.</div>
<a class="wotd-src" href="https://www.merriam-webster.com/word-of-the-day/baptism of fire-2026-08-11" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Merriam-Webster</a>
</div>

<div class="fact funfact" id="fun-fact">
<div class="fact-label">💡 Fact of the day</div>
<div class="fact-text">From 1777 until its admission to the United States in 1791, Vermont existed as a self-governing independent state. It had its own copper coins and operated its own postal service. <a class="fact-src" href="https://www.thefactsite.com/fact-of-the-day/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">The Fact Site&nbsp;&rarr;</a></div>
